## Recommended New Automation Assets

- Step: `Then('the app asks for the missing details instead of guessing for {string}') -> ChatPage.expectAppAsksForClarification(caseId)`
- Page Object: `ChatPage.expectAppAsksForClarification(caseId: string): Promise<void>`
- Helper: `utils/chatBehaviour.ts` `verifyChatAsksForClarification(page, caseId, question)` with Check A (wording cue, provisional TBD) and Check B (no SQL run).
- Locators: none.

## Risk & Collision Flags

- **Ambiguous-step risk:** the widened intent step is a regex anchored with `^...$` over a fixed list of phrases; it cannot collide with the AMB/UNS behaviour steps, which use different text. `bddgen` reports undefined or ambiguous steps and is part of Stage 7.
- **Scenario duplication:** none; every question is unique within its feature file (checked by script), so Playwright test titles do not collide.
- **Locator drift:** no new locators; the shared chat locators were built from the live page for REV-001 and are reused unchanged.
- **Live environment:** every scenario asks the production chat a question and uses tokens against the account's daily limit; a run of all 800 questions may hit that limit.
- **Provisional wording cues:** the behaviour check passes or fails on wording cue lists that the owner has not agreed (TBD); review the first run's verdicts before trusting the pass rate.

## Traceability Cross-Check

- Every normalized case AMB-001 to AMB-100 in docs/normalizer/007.md received a classification (100 of 100).
- Every scenario in features/ambiguous-questions/007.feature carries exactly one case tag (AMB-001 to AMB-100) and maps to one normalized case; no orphan scenarios.
- Req ID is TBD for every case, so the Req ID link cannot be checked.
